IGUI_KBW_GuideAboutBody::en = " <H2> A passion project for builders <TEXT> <LINE> <LINE> I have always wanted Project Zomboid to offer a building experience where a large collection stays organized, choices are easy to understand, and an entire base can be planned before spending the first plank. That idea began with my Building Menu for Build 41. <LINE> <LINE> Build 42 changed construction and introduced a new vanilla Building Menu, but the planning tools, grouped choices, detailed recipe information, flexible presentation, and multiplayer controls I wanted were still outside what the vanilla menu offered. Rather than leave those ideas behind, I started again and built Knox Buildworks around them. <LINE> <LINE> This mod is my attempt to make building feel like a complete part of the game: browse naturally, understand what a recipe will do, design large projects, share them with friends, and give server communities meaningful control without losing the fun of construction. <LINE> <LINE> <PUSHRGB:0.88,0.78,0.40> <SPACE> Knox Buildworks is made for the community and continues to grow through player feedback. <POPRGB> <SPACE> If you enjoy the mod and would like to support the time that goes into it, the button below opens my Ko-fi page. Support is always optional, and playing, reporting problems, and sharing the mod are appreciated too.",

Validate and Save must succeed before the server publishes a new revision. <LINE> <LINE> <H2> Editor layout <TEXT> <LINE> <LINE> Select a category, subcategory, or buildable on the left, then select a stage and tab on the right. Non-wall paintable objects keep their normal requirements. Reload discards the draft and requests current server data.
